# Downstream ML Pipeline Benchmark Summary: `SemDeDup` Coreset Pruning & Megapixel VQ

- **Suite 1 (`SemDeDup` & Coreset Selection) Runs**: `575` across `5` real labeled ML cells (`0` `NaN`/`Inf`, `100%` single-SKU per cell).
- **Suite 2 (`Megapixel VQ`) Runs**: `690` across `6` high-resolution RGB & `4x4x3` patch cells.
- **Cold-JIT Timing Spikes Detected**: `0`.
